- **spec-quality-auditor**: Adversarial, read-only auditor that grades the SPEC CONTENT in the current diff — for each acceptance criterion it hunts untestability, internal contradiction (read against the full current spec, so a new criterion that contradicts an unchanged one is caught), unstated edge/negative cases, gameability, and undocumented architecture/trade-off calls. Invoke from a pre-PR gate (e.g. /next-task §7) whenever a diff adds, edits, or renames a specs/*/spec.md, so a bad criterion is caught before any code is written against it. Returns PASS / FAIL with US#.AC# evidence. ([Chris-Graffagnino/creance](https://github.com/Chris-Graffagnino/creance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-quality-auditor.md))

- **spec-reviewer-executor**: PROACTIVELY 仕様書を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性・未定義要素・制約違反を検出して、誰が実装しても同じ結果になる決定論的仕様への書き換えを発行する仕様検証専門エージェント。 以下のいずれかに該当する場合に使用する。 - 仕様書（要件定義書 / 基本設計書 / 内部設計書 / API 仕様書 等）を検証し、曖昧性・非決定性を網羅的に検出したい - 仕様内の未定義要素（暗黙の前提・欠落条件・例外未定義）を抽出したい - 仕様が「誰が書いても同じ実装になる」レベルの決定論性を満たすか判定したい - 制約違反（ネットワーク障害・並行性・分散システム前提）を検出したい - 失敗定義（失敗種別・検出方法・システム挙動）の網羅性を検証したい - 仕様を決定論的状態（曖昧性 0 / 単一実装 / 計測可能値）に書き換えたい - 保証境界（保証対象 / 非保証対象 / 条件）を明示化したい - 仕様の最終判定（決定論的 / テスト可能 / 実装可能）を取得したい 使用しないケース：仕様の作成・抽出そのもの（→ spec-items-clarifier スキル）／コード実装の検証（→ code-review-executor）／設計判断のみで仕様文書が存在しない場合（→ system-basic-design / clean-architecture スキル）／仕様書の単純な構文・誤字修正のみ… ([YSHK721/chart](https://github.com/YSHK721/chart/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer-executor.md))
- **spec-reviewer-ko**: implementer-ko 산출 코드가 acceptance-criteria.md 와 spec.md 에 정확히 부합하는지 검증하는 specops-ko Phase B Evaluator. 코드 품질이 아닌 "스펙 준수" 만 평가. ([andyko18/devFSD](https://github.com/andyko18/devFSD/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer-ko.md))

- **speckit-plan**: Create `.specify/specs/*/plan.md` from an existing feature spec. ([boussadjra/vuestrata](https://github.com/boussadjra/vuestrata/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/speckit-plan.md) · ★111)
- **speckit-plan**: Implementation planning agent. Creates technical plans with research on libraries, APIs, and best practices. PRIMARY research agent. ([rolandzwaga/krate-audio](https://github.com/rolandzwaga/krate-audio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/speckit-plan.md) · ★9)
- **speckit-plan**: Execute the implementation planning workflow using the plan template to generate design artifacts. ([chenqin231/kcptun](https://github.com/chenqin231/kcptun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/speckit-plan.md) · ★1)
- **speckit-research**: Standalone technical research. Use when uncertain about technology selection, architecture design, or technical feasibility. Produces structured research.md. ([chenqin231/kcptun](https://github.com/chenqin231/kcptun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/speckit-research.md) · ★1)

- **speckit-skill-reviewer**: Focused, fast review of a single changed SKILL.md and its optional Codex mirror. Checks frontmatter validity, trigger-phrase quality (the description is what Claude matches on), references/scripts split conventions, and Codex mirror parity. Cheaper than Layer 2/3 evals — use as a pre-commit gate; reserve Layer 2/3 for CI / pre-release. Spawn with the file path of the SKILL.md being reviewed. ([racecraft-lab/racecraft-plugins-public](https://github.com/racecraft-lab/racecraft-plugins-public/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/speckit-skill-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **speckit-spec-author**: Use this when the user wants a github/spec-kit style spec for a feature (mentions "spec-kit", "/specify", "structured spec", or wants to scaffold .specify/ artifacts). Produces specs/&lt;NNN-feature-slug&gt;/spec.md with the spec-kit headings — Overview, Requirements (FR-### / NFR-###), User Scenarios, Edge Cases, Out of Scope, Open Questions. Expect: a numbered spec directory ready to feed into /plan and /tasks. ([DuganLabs/BaseNative](https://github.com/DuganLabs/BaseNative/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/speckit-spec-author.md) · 2 copies)
